Existing approaches typically generate such explanations using auxiliary models, including generative adversarial networks and diffusion models. While often capable of producing visually realistic images, these methods explain one black-box model using another, making it difficult to separate the classifier's decision-making process from the inductive biases of the generator.
  We propose a novel counterfactual-generation framework that requires no generative model. Instead, counterfactuals are constructed directly from causal evidence extracted from the classifier. The resulting approach is deterministic, requires no additional model training, and enables controllable edits within user-specified regions of interest. Experiments on real-world medical imaging datasets demonstrate that the proposed method successfully changes classifier predictions while remaining closer to the original image than generative baselines, providing a more direct and transparent view of the classifier's decision boundary.

</details>


### [104] [A Top-Down Framework for Metric-Scale Athlete Localization from Single Broadcast Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02705)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Phuc Nguyen,Linh-Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从单帧校准广播画面中进行度量级运动员世界坐标定位的top-down框架，通过边界感知自适应切片（Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling）解决超高分辨率图像中的极端尺度差异问题，并利用改进的RTMPose-X双关键点估计器结合相机标定射线投射实现定位，最终在公开测试集上取得LocSim 97.44和mAP 0.9128的成绩，较基线提升超过21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧广播视频中运动员的世界坐标精确本地化极具挑战性，主要源于超高分辨率图像中极端尺度差异导致的目标被边界切分、检测召回率下降等问题，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: （1）提出边界感知自适应切片（Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling），基于粗粒度包围盒预测迭代扩展切片边界，确保目标完整包含，无需架构改动即可缓解边界切分伪影；（2）将RTMPose-X架构适配为专用双关键点估计器（盆骨和地面投影点），采用重新设计的门控注意力单元优化这对几何耦合点；（3）通过相机标定射线投射将2D地面投影点确定性提升至世界坐标。

Result: 在公开测试集上达到LocSim 97.44和mAP 0.9128，较基线提升超过21%，验证了该方法在高分辨率尺度变化场景下的稳健性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效隔离了透视畸变作为残差定位误差的主要来源，为高分辨率广播画面中的运动员度量级定位提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate world-coordinate localization of athletes from single-frame broadcast footage is inherently challenging due to extreme scale disparities in ultra-high-resolution imagery. In this paper, we propose a top-down framework for metric-scale athlete localization from a single calibrated frame. Our approach centers on three key contributions. First, we propose Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling, a semantics-guided extension of standard sliced inference. By iteratively expanding tile boundaries based on coarse bounding-box predictions, it systematically ensures full object containment, effectively mitigating boundary-splitting artifacts through a lightweight pipeline adaptation without architectural modifications. By substantially mitigating recall degradation under extreme scale variance, Boundary-Aware Adaptive Tiling enables us to isolate perspective distortion as the primary source of residual localization error. Second, we adapt the RTMPose-X architecture into a specialized two-keypoint estimator (pelvis and ground projection), employing a reformulated Gated Attention Unit optimized for this geometrically coupled point pair, and then deterministically lift the 2D ground projections into world coordinates via camera-calibrated ray casting. On the public test set, our method achieves a LocSim score of 97.44 and an mAP of 0.9128, outperforming the baseline by over 21 \% and establishing a robust solution for high-resolution scale variance.

</details>


### [105] [InceptionGS: Generative Bootstrapping for Large-Scale Gaussian Splatting under Unstructured View Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02747)
*Tianheng Lu,Guangyu Wang,Ruqi Huang,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InceptionGS，一种通过平衡重建与生成来增强高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）的方法，用于在大规模场景中处理因视角稀疏导致的不充分观测区域，同时保持其他区域的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模场景数字化需要从所有视角实现一致且美观的渲染，但采集覆盖每个细节的多视角图像因场景复杂度、成本等因素不可行，导致视角高度非结构化——大部分区域覆盖良好但某些区域观测不足。现有基于重建的方法对视角稀缺敏感，而基于生成的方法存在泛化、可控性和3D一致性问题。

Method: InceptionGS从一个初始的高斯泼溅开始，通过软性地融入场景自适应和视角自适应的生成先验，合理地重新思考并修复因视角稀缺导致的问题区域，同时保持其他区域的质量，从而在重建与生成之间取得精妙的平衡。

Result: 在真实世界的大规模场景上的广泛实验证明了该方法在处理非结构化图像和提升高保真高斯泼溅方面的优越性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: InceptionGS通过巧妙地结合重建与生成策略，有效解决了大规模场景中视角稀缺条件下的渲染质量问题，为实际场景数字化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Achieving truly immersive large-scale scene digitization necessitates consistent and visually pleasing rendering across all possible viewing perspectives. However, collecting multi-view images covering every fine detail of a large-scale scene is prohibitive due to scene complexity, capture cost, negligence, or accessibility constraints. As a result, the sampled views tend to be highly unstructured -- the majority of the scene is well covered yet certain regions inevitably lack sufficient observations. Existing reconstruction based methods are vulnerable to view scarcity while generation based approaches suffer from generalization, controllability, and 3D consistency issues. To address this challenge, we propose InceptionGS, which bootstraps Gaussian splatting by subtly balancing reconstruction and generation. Starting from an initial Gaussian splatting, InceptionGS reasonably rethinks and repairs problematic regions caused by view scarcity while preserving the quality elsewhere, by softly incorporating scene- and view-adaptive generative priors. Extensive experiments on real-world large-scale scenes demonstrate the superiority and broad applicability of our approach in handling unstructured imagery and boosting high-fidelity Gaussian splatting. Please refer to the supplementary video for better visual demonstrations.

</details>


### [106] [Balancing Frequencies and Pixels in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02748)
*Lucas Degeorge,Paul Couairon,Arijit Ghosh,Alexei A. Efros,David Picard,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种频域平衡的损失函数f-loss，用于解决像素空间流模型训练中低频主导梯度、高频细节学习缓慢的问题。通过早期强调频域学习、后期转为像素空间v-loss的训练策略，在不改变架构的情况下加速收敛40%并提升FID和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像遵循1/f²频谱分布，而像素空间重构目标将所有空间误差一视同仁，导致低频占据优化主导信号，延迟了高频细节（纹理、边缘）的学习，造成训练效率低下。

Method: 提出Focal Log-Frequency Loss（f-loss）：一种频谱平衡的目标函数，均衡不同频率的学习信号，突出像素空间中未被充分表示的高频成分。在此基础上设计两阶段训练策略：早期侧重频域监督以捕获所有频率，后期切换到像素空间v-loss进行空间精修，作为流匹配损失的即插即用替代。

Result: 在多个模型规模下，加速收敛最高达40%，并持续改善FID和感知保真度指标。

Conclusion: 该方法概念简单、无需架构改动，通过平衡训练信号中的频域分布并随模型需求动态调整监督方式，有效缓解了像素空间损失的基频偏置问题，是一种实用且高效的训练改进方案。

Abstract: Natural images follow a $1/f^2$ spectral distribution: most signal energy lies in the low spatial frequencies, while the perceptually important structures such as textures and edges occupy sparse high-frequency bands. Pixel-space reconstruction objectives, however, treat all spatial errors uniformly, causing low frequencies to dominate the optimization signal and delaying the learning of fine-scale details. In this work, we identify this objective-level spectral imbalance as a key inefficiency in training pixel-space flow models. To address it, we propose a Focal Log-Frequency Loss (f-loss), a spectrally balanced objective that equalizes the learning signal across frequencies, emphasizing high-frequency components that are otherwise underrepresented in pixel-space objectives. Building on this, we introduce a simple training strategy that combines frequency and pixel supervision: we first emphasize frequency-domain learning early to capture all frequencies, and then transition to standard pixel-space v-loss for spatial refinement. This balancing mitigates the low-frequency bias of pixel losses and aligns the training signal with the evolving needs of the model. Our approach is conceptually simple, requires no architectural changes, and acts as a drop-in replacement for flow matching losses. Across multiple model scales, it accelerates convergence by up to 40% while consistently improving FID and perceptual fidelity. We will release code and models.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution in Facial Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02751)
*Sheng Liu,Qiang Sheng,Danding Wang,Yu Li,Chenming Zhou,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多工具图像编辑归因问题(MIEA)，构建了包含50万+编辑人脸图像的MultiEdit数据集，并设计了DPEC方法，从空间和频率域捕获可区分的、局部感知的编辑工具痕迹，配合基于错误的课程学习策略，在最多五步编辑的人脸图像上优于九种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑归因方法仅假设单一编辑工具，无法应对现实中常见的多工具编辑场景，其中不同编辑工具留下的痕迹是复合且重叠的。

Method: 构建MultiEdit数据集模拟真实人脸编辑操作，覆盖六种支持换脸和多种面部增强的编辑工具；通过数据分析设计DPEC方法，在空间域和频率域捕获可区分且局部感知的编辑工具痕迹，并采用基于错误的课程学习策略进行训练。

Result: DPEC在最多五步编辑的人脸图像上优于九种对比方法。

Conclusion: 本文填补了多工具编辑归因的研究空白，验证了从多域提取局部痕迹及课程学习策略在复合编辑痕迹归因上的有效性。

Abstract: As generative AI tools become increasingly powerful and easy to use, people can easily edit portrait images with a prompt, necessitating the task of image editing attribution, which predicts the involved editing tools from the given image. Existing attribution methods hold the single-tool assumption and can only attribute a specific editing tool, but struggle to handle the more complex and increasingly common multi-tool editing scenarios, where artifacts left by different editing tools are composite and overlapped. To address this gap, we explore Multi-Tool Image Editing Attribution (MIEA), which aims to identify multiple editing tools involved in a multi-tool edited facial image. To simulate the real-life editing operations on facial images, we then construct a new dataset, MultiEdit, which contains 500k+ edited facial images and covers six types of editing tools that support face swapping (Deepfake) and various facial enhancements. Inspired by the findings from data analysis, we design DPEC, a multi-tool attribution method that can capture distinguishable, locality-aware editing tool traces from both spatial and frequency domains with the support of an error-based curriculum learning strategy. Experiments show \Method\ outperforms nine methods for facial images edited in at most five steps.

</details>


### [108] [Video-Based Palm-Vein Authentication under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02776)
*Xiaofeng Yan,Kechen Liu,Abhilash Venkatesh,Cathy Zhang,Xia Zhou,Salvatore Stolfo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个公开的视频版掌静脉数据集CUP，覆盖清洁、热、湿、脏四种表面条件并配有生理/人口统计元数据。在基准测试中，21种识别器在脏手掌上性能大幅下降（EER约翻四倍）。作者设计了时间帧共识和基于显著性引导的区域级最优传输匹配方法，在不增加可学习参数的情况下有效恢复鲁棒性，并在4.3M参数和3.1 GFLOPs的低成本下全面领先各表面指标。附接到四个冻结骨干网络后平均EER降低29-37%，在四个公开单图像数据集上也有提升。初步审计还发现体水和性别相关的两个热条件性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掌静脉生物识别在实际部署中面临表面噪声（汗液、污垢）、光照与运动变化以及温度驱动的血管可见性变化，但缺乏在真实干扰条件下采集的数据来研究这些效应，因此作者构建了CUP数据集并系统研究其影响。

Method: 1）构建CUP视频掌静脉数据集，每种手掌在四种表面条件下录制并提供生理和人口统计元数据；2）基准测试21种静态、视频和多帧聚合识别架构；3）提出基于时间帧共识消除瞬态污染，以及结合全局余弦与显著性引导的区域级最优传输匹配的空间端测试时匹配方法，无需额外学习参数。

Result: CUP基准显示干净手掌上可靠的模型在脏手掌上准确率大幅下降（EER约翻四倍）。提出的方法在CUP所有表面上EER、TAR@FAR=0.01和Rank-1指标均领先，仅4.3M参数和3.1 GFLOPs（远低于视频模型）。附接到四个冻结SOTA骨干上平均EER降低29-37%，在四个公开单图像数据集上区域匹配仍有效。初步人口统计审计发现体水和性别上的两个热条件差距通过多重比较校正。

Conclusion: 掌静脉识别对表面污染的鲁棒性可通过时间一致性融合与空间显著性引导的区域匹配大幅恢复，且不需要重新训练；同时该研究揭示了需要关注的潜在公平性问题（不同人口群体在不同条件下的性能差异）。CUP数据集将开源供非商业研究使用。

Abstract: Palm-vein biometrics are increasingly used for secure, contactless authentication. Yet real-world deployment exposes them to surface noise (sweat, dirt), illumination and motion variation, and temperature-driven changes in vascular visibility, which remain underexplored for lack of data captured under such conditions. To study these effects, we introduce the Columbia University Palm-vein (CUP) dataset, to our knowledge the first public video-based palm-vein dataset. CUP records every palm under four surface conditions (a clean baseline, warm, wet, and dirty) and pairs each subject with physiological and demographic metadata. On it we benchmark twenty-one recognizers spanning static, video, and multi-frame aggregation architectures. Models that verify reliably on clean palms lose most of their accuracy on dirty ones, and the mean equal error rate (EER) roughly quadruples. We recover much of that robustness along both axes of the capture. Temporally, a consensus over the few frames the sensor already returns cancels transient corruption; spatially, a test-time matcher that adds no learned parameters fuses the global cosine with a saliency-steered region-level optimal transport that routes the comparison around corrupted regions. The full design leads on every surface of CUP in EER, TAR@FAR=0.01, and Rank-1, at 4.3M parameters and 3.1 GFLOPs, a fraction of the video models' cost. Attached to four frozen state-of-the-art backbones it cuts their mean EER by 29-37% without retraining, and on four public single-image datasets the regional matching alone still helps. A preliminary audit across ten demographic and physiological traits finds two warm-condition gaps, along body water and gender, that survive multiple-comparison correction. CUP will be released for non-commercial research use at https://github.com/MobileX-CU/CUP_v1 upon publication.

</details>


### [109] [ShallowStream: Index Shallow then Answer Deep for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02780)
*Jitai Hao,Ke Yang,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShallowStream是一个利用多模态大语言模型浅层进行视频流理解的框架，通过浅层KV缓存构建轻量级索引和注意力分数检索证据，在保持与最先进流式方法相当性能的同时，将每帧预填充延迟和10秒端到端延迟分别降低最高52.1倍和11.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解在现实应用中至关重要，但使用MLLMs处理连续视频流计算开销巨大。现有方法大多忽视了模型深度这一维度，对每个输入帧都重复执行全深度预填充，导致高昂的计算开销以及KV缓存随预填充深度线性增长的问题。

Method: ShallowStream利用MLLM的浅层同时完成帧编码和检索索引构建。在流处理过程中，使用浅层KV缓存维护始终在线的轻量级索引；在查询回答时，利用浅层生成的注意力分数对上下文帧进行评分，并采用多样性感知的选择策略检索精确且全面的证据。

Result: ShallowStream在性能上与现有最强的流式方法相当，同时将每帧预填充延迟降低最多52.1倍，10秒端到端延迟降低最多11.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过利用浅层去处理流式帧编码与索引构建，ShallowStream有效解决了全深度预填充的计算瓶颈，为流式视频理解提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding is a critical capability for real-world applications, including embodied intelligence, autonomous driving, industrial monitoring, surveillance and early warning, and wearable assistants. However, processing continuous video streams with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is computationally expensive. Existing efforts have explored reducing streaming overhead through visual token pruning, token merging, quantization, on-demand frame retrieval, and context offloading. However, most existing methods overlook the dimension of model depth. Repeatedly executing full-depth MLLM prefill over incoming frames is prohibitively expensive, incurring substantial computational overhead and causing the KV cache to grow at a rate directly proportional to the prefill depth. To address these challenges, we propose ShallowStream, a novel framework that leverages the shallow layers of an MLLM to simultaneously perform frame encoding and retrieval index building. During stream processing, ShallowStream maintains an always-on lightweight index using the KV cache of shallow layers. During query-time answering, we leverage the attention scores generated by the shallow layers to score context frames and employ a diversity-aware selection strategy to retrieve precise and comprehensive evidence. ShallowStream achieves performance on par with the strongest existing streaming methods, while reducing per-frame prefill latency and 10-second end-to-end latency by up to 52.1x and 11.9x, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/CURRENTF/ShallowStream.

</details>


### [110] [AutoCompass: Accurate Visual Localization on Public Maps by Learning from Weak Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02798)
*Javier Tirado-Garín,Alan Savio Paul,Shuai Chen,Axel Barroso-Laguna,Tommaso Cavallari,Daniyar Turmukhambetov,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Eric Brachmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AutoCompass监督方法，用于在标签不精确的情况下训练神经地图匹配器，通过去除航向标签依赖、定义GPS容差区域以及利用SLAM/SfM相对位姿，显著优于传统依赖绝对位姿标签的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经地图匹配器通常依赖大规模地理参考图像数据集训练，但其中的位置和航向标注常含有噪声，严重影响模型训练的精度和鲁棒性。

Method: 三种核心策略：(1) 仅使用原始GPS标签训练，让模型自动学习预测准确航向；(2) 在原始GPS周围定义容差区域以提高位置精度；(3) 利用SLAM或SfM获得的训练图像间相对位姿，提供更精确的训练信号。

Result: 在驾驶和以自我为中心的基准测试中，AutoCompass一致优于使用强依赖绝对位姿标签训练的对比方法。

Conclusion: AutoCompass证明无需精确的绝对位姿标签也能有效训练神经地图匹配器，为处理现实世界含噪标注数据提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Neural map matchers estimate an image's 3-DoF pose relative to a 2D map. These models are trained on large-scale datasets of geo-referenced images, whose position and heading labels often contain noise that affects the trained models. To address this, we present AutoCompass, a supervision approach for training neural map matchers from inaccurate absolute pose labels. First, we show that heading labels are unnecessary: trained from raw GPS labels, models learn to predict accurate headings, automatically. Second, defining a tolerance region around raw GPS improves positional accuracy. Third, if available, our supervision uses relative poses between training images, obtained via SLAM or SfM, which provide a more accurate training signal. Across driving and egocentric benchmarks, AutoCompass consistently outperforms counterparts trained with the usual strong reliance on absolute pose labels.

</details>


### [111] [GDB-Reward: From Evaluation Metrics to Training Rewards for Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02813)
*Adrienne Deganutti,Purvanshi Mehta,Simon Hadfield,Andrew Gilbert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GDB-Reward框架，将异构的图形设计评估指标系统性地转化为统一的强化学习奖励，在冻结图像生成器的情况下显著提升设计规范遵从度（包括感知质量、渲染保真度和空间准确性）。研究证明异构的非可微分评估指标可以超越被动基准测试，成为强化学习的有效优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图模型在自然图像合成中表现出色，但在图形设计领域面临挑战，因为设计成功依赖于排版、布局、颜色和视觉传达等精确约束。提示优化是微调昂贵扩散模型的有吸引力的替代方案，但学习冻结图像生成器的提示需要信息丰富的奖励函数，而生成过程完全不可微分。这促使研究者探索是否可以将设计评估指标本身作为强化学习的奖励。

Method: 核心贡献是GDB-Reward框架，它系统性地将异构的图形设计评估指标（涵盖感知质量、渲染保真度和空间准确性等维度）转化为统一的强化学习奖励信号，用于优化提示词，同时保持图像生成器完全冻结。

Result: 实验表明，GDB-Reward作为优化目标，能显著提高对设计规范的遵从度，在感知质量、渲染保真度和空间准确性方面均有大幅改善，且图像生成器无需任何微调。

Conclusion: 该工作证明异构的非可微分评估指标可以超越被动基准测试的角色，成为强化学习的有效优化目标，尤其适用于缺乏可微分监督的领域。这为图形设计生成以及更广泛的不可微分评估场景提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Text-to-image models excel at natural image synthesis but struggle with graphic design, where success depends on satisfying precise constraints on typography, layout, color, and visual communication. While prompt optimization offers an attractive alternative to expensive diffusion model fine-tuning, learning prompts for frozen image generators requires informative reward functions despite the entirely non-differentiable generation process. Reinforcement learning does not require differentiable objectives; it requires only scalar rewards capable of ranking candidate outputs. This raises a simple question: can design evaluation metrics themselves become reinforcement learning rewards? Our central contribution is GDB-Reward, a framework that systematically transforms heterogeneous graphic design evaluation metrics into a unified reinforcement learning reward. Experiments demonstrate that GDB-Reward provides an effective optimization objective, substantially improving adherence to the design specification in perceptual quality, rendering fidelity, and spatial accuracy while keeping the image generator entirely frozen. More broadly, our results demonstrate that heterogeneous, non-differentiable evaluation metrics can move beyond passive benchmarking to become effective optimization objectives for reinforcement learning in domains where differentiable supervision is unavailable.

</details>


### [112] [Benchmarking RAW and RGB Restoration in Image Signal Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02831)
*Zihao Lu,Radu Timofte,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了盲恢复围绕固定ISP的两种放置方案：RAW域（ISP前）恢复与sRGB域（ISP后）恢复，覆盖多种智能手机设备组、学习型ISP和退化类型。结论是放置位置本身不决定性能，RAW恢复优于通用RGB模型，但经过ISP感知训练的RGB模型整体性能最佳，恢复性能强烈取决于模型与成像管线的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机通过ISP将RAW传感器数据转换为sRGB图像，而盲图像恢复任务在ISP之前（RAW域）或之后（sRGB域）的放置选择缺乏系统性的基准评估，研究者希望弄清哪种恢复策略更优及其决定因素。

Method: 构建了一个综合基准，包含四种智能手机设备组、两种学习型ISP、三种退化场景（噪声、模糊、噪声与模糊联合），对比RAW域恢复模型、通用RGB恢复模型以及ISP感知训练的RGB恢复模型的性能。

Result: 放置位置本身并不单独决定性能；RAW恢复策略优于最佳通用RGB恢复模型；但考虑ISP变换训练的RGB恢复模型实现了最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: 图像重建性能强烈依赖于恢复模型与目标成像管线的对齐程度，建议将恢复放置位置和ISP感知监督作为关键实验因素进行报告。

Abstract: Modern cameras transform RAW sensor measurements into sRGB images through an image signal processor (ISP). We benchmark two placements for blind restoration around a fixed ISP: (A) pre-ISP restoration in the RAW domain and (B) post-ISP restoration in the sRGB domain. The benchmark covers four smartphone device groups, two learned ISPs, three degradation regimes--noise, blur, and joint noise and blur--, and several representative RAW and RGB restoration models. Our results show that placement alone does not determine performance. The RAW restoration strategy outperforms the best generic RGB restoration models. However, RGB restoration models trained considering the ISP transformations, achieve the best overall performance. Our novel benchmark demonstrates that the image reconstruction performance strongly depends on the alignment between the restoration model and the target imaging pipeline. We consequently recommend reporting restoration placement and ISP-aware supervision as key experimental factors. Our code is available at https://github.com/mv-lab/AISP

</details>


### [113] [Efficient All-in-One Weather Restoration using Spectral Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02839)
*Paula Garrido-Mellado,Daniel Feijoo,Yuning Cui,Alvaro Garcia,Marcos V. Conde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FReSH-IR，一种轻量级全场景图像复原方法，通过频谱谐波化将特征分解为高低频分量，利用傅里叶跳跃连接整合频域与空间信息，在保持与Transformer模型相当复原质量的同时减少80%的参数和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气（雨、雾、雪）会严重降低图像质量，影响人类感知和物理AI系统。现有复原方法计算开销大，难以处理高分辨率图像并应对多种不同退化类型。

Method: FReSH-IR在层次化编码器-解码器架构的每一尺度显式地将特征表示分解为高频和低频分量，并通过基于傅里叶的跳跃连接将频谱分解与空间处理相结合，以捕获互补的频率信息而不损失空间细节。

Result: 该方法在达到与基于Transformer的模型相似的复原质量的同时，参数和操作数量减少了80%，在效率与性能之间实现了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: 大量实验表明，FReSH-IR提供了出色的效率-性能权衡，突显了其在资源受限系统中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Adverse weather conditions such as rain, haze, and snow significantly degrade image quality, posing challenges for both human perception and physical AI. Existing restoration methods require large computational budgets, struggling to process high-resolution images and handle different degradations. In this paper, we present Frequency Reconstruction via Spectral Harmonization, a novel lightweight all-in-one restoration method that explicitly decomposes feature representations into high- and low-frequency components at each scale of a hierarchical encoder-decoder architecture. By combining spectral decomposition with spatial processing through Fourier-based skip connections, FReSH-IR captures complementary frequency information without sacrificing spatial detail. Our approach achieves similar restoration quality with 80% fewer parameters and operations than transformer-based models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method offers a great efficiency-performance trade-off, highlighting its practical applications in constrained-resource systems.

</details>


### [114] [RoGe: Novel View Synthesis via End-to-End Implicit Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02847)
*Xiaolei Lang,Ze Kang,Zehao Huang,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoGe是一个端到端的重建与生成统一框架，用于稀疏视角场景漫游。它通过前馈重建模型构建隐式场景表示，用目标相机光线查询得到几何特征，直接注入视频扩散模型作为条件，无需任何显式3D中间表示，且两模块联合训练使生成目标直接塑造几何条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合方法用渲染图像或显式3D表示（点云、3D高斯）在重建与生成之间搭建桥梁，但生成过程受限于有损且不完美的场景投影而继承了其误差，且重建无法从生成获得修正信号，因此需要移除这一显式桥梁的端到端统一框架。

Method: RoGe包含两部分：(1) 前馈重建模型从稀疏输入视图构建隐式场景表示；(2) 用目标相机光线查询该表示，获取逐视角几何特征并注入视频扩散模型作为条件。全程无3D中间表示，重建与生成模块联合训练。

Result: 在DL3DV数据集上，RoGe在图像级指标和视频级时间一致性上均优于基于重建、基于生成以及混合方法的基线。消融实验表明光线查询的隐式特征优于原始重建token和渲染RGB作为条件，联合训练带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 移除显式3D桥梁、以光线查询的隐式特征作为生成条件并联合训练，是稀疏视角新视图合成中打通重建与生成的有效范式，能够同时提升图像质量和时间一致性。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse inputs requires both geometric grounding from the observed views and generative priors of unobserved regions, motivating recent hybrid methods that combine reconstruction and generation. However, existing methods bridge the two with rendered images or explicit 3D representations such as point maps or 3D Gaussians. Generation is thus conditioned on a lossy and imperfect projection of the scene, inheriting its errors, and reconstruction receives no signal from generation to correct them. We present RoGe, an end-to-end unified reconstruction and generation framework that removes this explicit bridge. It targets roaming within a scene anchored by sparse views: given a few posed images and a camera trajectory, it synthesizes a temporally coherent video along that trajectory. From the sparse input views, RoGe builds an implicit scene representation with a feed-forward reconstruction model, and queries it with target camera rays to obtain per-view geometric features. These features are injected into a video diffusion model as conditioning, without any 3D intermediate. Both modules are trained jointly, so the generation objective directly shapes its own geometric conditioning. We conduct experiments on DL3DV, where RoGe outperforms reconstruction-based, generation-based, and hybrid baselines on image-level metrics and video-level temporal consistency. Ablations confirm that ray-queried implicit features outperform both raw reconstruction tokens and rendered RGB as conditioning, and that joint training brings further gains.

</details>


### [115] [MuyBridge: Mobile Human Center-of-Mass Estimation from Monocular Video via Sparse Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02854)
*Aidan Bradshaw,Marco Giordano,David Rode,Andreas Habersack,Elif Basokur,Annika Kruse,Markus Tilp,Michele Magno,Peter Wolf,Luca Benini,Christoph Leitner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuyBridge，一种基于单目手机摄像头实时估计运动员3D质心轨迹的端到端系统，通过紧凑2D姿态网络与蒸馏单目深度网络结合解析度量融合，利用解剖与物理先验锚定公制质心，无需3D或任务特定监督。在AthletePose3D数据集上实现了33-41mm垂直质心误差和2.3-6.6%范围误差，可在iPhone 15上63 FPS运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D质心是运动、康复和临床生物力学分析的核心量，但现有方法要么只优化3D关键点精度而缺乏解剖约束，要么需要过重的计算和采集基础设施。教练和运动分析师难以在训练和比赛现场用单摄像头测量公制质心，因此需要轻量、可部署的单相机质心追踪方案。

Method: MuyBridge由三部分组成：紧凑2D姿态网络、蒸馏的单步单目深度网络，以及解析度量融合模块。融合模块利用解剖学与物理学先验将公制质心锚定到节段级位置，系统采用一次性校准即可运行，2D姿态与深度网络以异步方式协同工作以保证质心估计效率。

Result: 在AthletePose3D的跑步、田径和花样滑冰数据集上，MuyBridge实现了33-41mm的垂直质心误差和2.3-6.6%的绝对相对范围误差；在iPhone 15上，以63 FPS的姿态估计速率产出质心估计，深度更新异步为2.86 Hz。

Conclusion: MuyBridge首次实现了从单一手机摄像头视频流进行运动员节段质心轨迹的实时估计，将高精度质心测量从实验室约束中解放出来，使得教练和运动分析师能够在训练和比赛环境中直接使用公制质心指标。系统无需3D监督即可运行，性能与设备端实时的能力使其具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: The 3D center of mass (CoM) is a primary quantity in the biomechanical analysis of sport, rehabilitation, and clinical movement, yet existing 3D pose tracking, mesh recovery, and multi-view triangulation methods either optimize 3D keypoint accuracy without anatomical constraints or carry compute and capture infrastructure too heavy to deploy where CoM tracking is most useful. As a result, the metric CoM remains difficult for coaches and movement analysts to measure from a single camera where athletes train and compete. In this work, we introduce MuyBridge, an on-device system that estimates the athlete's segmental center of mass trajectory from a single phone camera video stream. MuyBridge couples a compact 2D pose network and a distilled single-step monocular depth network through an analytic metric fusion that uses anatomical and physical priors to anchor the metric CoM, requiring no 3D or task-specific supervision. Evaluated on the athletic movements of AthletePose3D (running, track and field, and figure skating), MuyBridge achieves 33-41 mm vertical CoM error and 2.3-6.6% absolute-relative range error (AbsRel) under a one-time calibration, and produces CoM estimates at the 63 FPS pose-estimation rate using asynchronous 2.86 Hz depth updates on iPhone 15. Code is available at: https://github.com/Abradshaw1/Muybridge

</details>


### [116] [PlantC2USeg: Cross-Scale Consistent Pre-Training for Few-Shot Unified Plant Point Cloud Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02860)
*Yu Tian,Xintong Jiang,Jan Franklin Adamowski,Shiv O. Prasher,Shangpeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlantC2USeg，一个用于植物点云分割的深度迁移学习框架，通过跨尺度一致性学习和信息受限解码策略实现少样本跨域泛化。在多个数据集上（Soybean3D、HR3D、SYAU-Maize和ShapeNet Part）均取得领先性能，大幅降低标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代作物育种需要精确的器官级分析，但传统深度学习依赖大量密集标注数据，获取成本高昂。在分布偏移（不同物种、不同传感条件）下实现统一的植物点云分割且只需极少量额外训练样本，仍是一个挑战性难题。

Method: 提出PlantC2USeg深度迁移学习框架，包含两个关键组件：(1)跨尺度一致性学习，显式对齐不同空间尺度下的特征；(2)信息受限解码策略，防止重建捷径并促进稳健适应。配合预训练实现跨物种和传感条件的少样本泛化，统一微调使用继承阈值以降低适应开销。

Result: 在Soybean3D全监督下达到91.91%语义IoU和94.62%实例mWCov；20个标注样本下为89.78%和90.27%；仅10个样本仍保持83.23% mWCov和83.19% IoU。HR3D上10-shot迁移平均78.41% IoU和79.42% mWCov，SYAU-Maize 22-shot达92.75% IoU和93.51% mRec。ShapeNet Part上mIoU达85.0%，展示了农业外的泛化能力。

Conclusion: PlantC2USeg显著降低了分布偏移下的适应成本，实现可扩展的植物表型分析和超越农业领域的可迁移3D表示学习，为少样本点云分割提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern crop breeding demands precise organ-level analysis for trait quantification, making plant point cloud segmentation (PPCS) increasingly important. However, conventional deep learning approaches rely heavily on densely annotated datasets that are labor-intensive to acquire. Unified PPCS adaptation from distribution-shifted examples with minimal additional training remains challenging. To address this, we propose PlantC2USeg, a deep transfer learning framework featuring cross-scale consistency learning to explicitly align features across spatial scales and an information-restricted decoding strategy that prevents reconstruction shortcuts and promotes robust adaptation. The resulting pre-training enables stable few-shot generalization across species and sensing conditions, while unified fine-tuning with inherited thresholds further reduces adaptation overhead. Under full supervision on Soybean3D, PlantC2USeg achieves the highest semantic IoU and instance mWCov among compared methods, at 91.91% and 94.62%. With 20 labeled samples, it leads both metrics at 89.78% and 90.27%; with only 10 samples, it retains the highest mWCov of 83.23% while achieving 83.19% IoU. Across HR3D, 10-shot transfer to tobacco, tomato, and sorghum averages 78.41% IoU and 79.42% mWCov, while 22-shot transfer to SYAU-Maize achieves the highest IoU and mRec at 92.75% and 93.51%. Furthermore, a leading category-averaged mIoU of 85.0% on ShapeNet Part demonstrates the framework's capability to handle diverse shape variations beyond agricultural domains. These results demonstrate that PlantC2USeg reduces overall adaptation effort under distribution shifts, enabling scalable plant phenotyping and transferable 3D representation learning beyond agriculture.

</details>


### [117] [Thinking in Pictures: A Systematic Benchmark for Reasoning-driven Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02864)
*Yutong Liu,Nan Huang,Xu Cao,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RIG-BENCH基准，系统评估统一生成模型在"推理驱动图像生成"（RIG）任务中的能力，涵盖概念、变换、模式结构与场景四类高认知需求领域，通过2000个精选样本揭示现有模型存在严重的"推理-生成差距"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一生成模型和世界模拟器虽然取得了显著的视觉感知和合成成果，但主要依赖表面级事件对齐，缺乏高层视觉推理能力。真正的视觉生成智能需要从"生成"走向"推理到生成"，因此需要一个系统性的基准来评估和诊断这一能力缺口。

Method: 构建了RIG-BENCH基准，包含2000个精选样本，从四个认知密集型维度系统评估推理驱动图像生成能力：基于概念的推理、基于变换的推理、模式与结构推理、基于场景的推理。

Result: 对最先进的统一生成模型和图像/视频生成模型的广泛评估揭示了显著的推理-生成差距：模型经常生成局部合理但全局不合逻辑的输出。

Conclusion: RIG-BENCH为开发下一代具有逻辑基础的统一生成模型和世界模拟器提供了重要的诊断框架和指导方向。

Abstract: Recent advancements in unified generative models (UGMs) and world simulators have achieved unprecedented results in visual perception and synthesis. However, these models primarily rely on surface-level event alignment, leaving the capacity for high-level visual reasoning underexplored. True visual generative intelligence demands "Reasoning-to-Generation", an ability to infer latent rules from visual inputs and manifest solutions through precise, logically constrained visual outcomes. We introduce RIG-BENCH, a novel comprehensive benchmark that systematically evaluates Reasoning-driven Image Generation (RIG) across four cognitively demanding domains: Concept-based, Transformation-based, Pattern & Structure, and Scenario-based. Featuring 2000 curated samples, RIG-BENCH serves as a rigorous stress test for RIG. Our extensive evaluations of state-of-the-art UGMs and image/video generation models reveal a significant reasoning-generation gap, wherein models frequently produce locally plausible but globally illogical outputs. RIG-BENCH provides a vital diagnostic framework to guide the development of next-generation, logically grounded UGMs and world simulators.

</details>


### [118] [SolarWM: Open Data and Scalable Training for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02886)
*Junchao Huang,Guian Fang,Shengju Qian,Xianghao Kong,Zhuoran Zhao,Wei Huang,Yihua Du,Zixin Zhang,Justin Cui,Yuchao Gu,Yukang Chen,Xinting Hu,Tianyu He,Shaoshuai Shi,Zhuotao Tian,Xin Wang,Mike Zheng Shou,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SolarWM是一个全开放的基础框架，用于构建交互式视频世界模型，涵盖了从数据准备到长时程推理的完整流程。它通过可重构的多源数据引擎和骨干网络原生适配框架，统一了来自10个数据集的143万条片段，并基于Wan2.2、LTX-2.5和MiniMax-H3实例化了四个5B-33B规模的因果模型，使模型仅需在5秒序列上训练即可实现分钟到小时级别的实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据源和视频骨干网络在时间尺度、相机几何、视觉质量、运动及字幕风格上存在显著差异，而不同视频生成器使用不同的表征和架构。直接混合数据或进行模型特定实现会导致监督信号不一致，结果难以复现和比较。

Method: 提出了两类核心组件：(1)可重构多源数据引擎，将10个数据集的143万条经典片段转换为统一的帧对齐契约，涵盖视觉观察、度量相机几何、字幕、质量元数据、选择决策和来源信息，将源处理与混合构建解耦；(2)骨干网络原生适配框架，在共享的相机条件、训练和推理接口下，保留Wan2.2、LTX-2.5和MiniMax-H3的原生表征与目标函数。采用统一的三阶段训练流程：双向适应、教师强制的自回归初始化以及分布匹配蒸馏。

Result: 成功实例化了四个5B-33B规模的因果交互式世界模型，基于三个不同骨干网络构建，模型在仅5秒序列上训练后即可实现实时交互，支持分钟至小时级别的rollout时长。

Conclusion: 通过发布完整的数据、流水线、训练配方、模型权重和框架，SolarWM为交互式世界模型研究提供了可复现且可扩展的基础，解决了异构数据与模型之间的耦合问题。

Abstract: We introduce SolarWM, a fully open foundation for building interactive video world models from data preparation through long-horizon inference. Training across heterogeneous data sources and video backbones is challenging: datasets differ in temporal scale, camera geometry, visual quality, motion, and captioning styles, while video generators use distinct representations and architectures. Naive data mixing and model-specific implementations therefore produce inconsistent supervision and make results difficult to reproduce and compare. SolarWM addresses this coupling with a reconfigurable multi-source data engine and a backbone-native adaptation framework. The engine converts 1.43 million canonical clips from 10 datasets into a unified, frame-aligned contract covering visual observations, metric camera geometry, captions, quality metadata, selection decisions, and provenance, while decoupling source processing from mixture construction. Under shared camera-conditioning, training, and inference interfaces, we instantiate four 5B--33B models based on Wan2.2, LTX-2.5, and MiniMax-H3 while preserving their native representations and objectives. A unified three-stage recipe combines bidirectional adaptation, teacher-forced autoregressive initialization, and distribution matching distillation. The resulting causal models enable real-time interaction over rollouts ranging from minutes to hours after being trained on only 5s sequences. By releasing the resulting data, pipeline, recipes, weights, and framework, SolarWM provides a reproducible and extensible foundation for interactive world-model research.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalDetectBench，一个开源pipeline和基准，用于衡量前沿大语言模型的"评估意识"（即模型识别自身正在被评估的能力），并指出现有文献中两个导致系统性偏差的方法论问题及其修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM能够识别自己被评估时会表现异常，从而破坏评估结果的有效性，威胁AI安全框架。现有研究存在方法论偏差，需要更可靠的基准来测量评估意识。

Method: EvalDetectBench是一个开源pipeline和基准，兼容Inspect评估，包含新构建的转录数据集（覆盖前沿系统卡评估和多样化的部署来源），并通过每模型探针校准和分层生成器协调程序修正两个偏差源：部署转录生成模型的身份（占11.25%方差）和elicitation提示在不同模型上的性能差异。

Result: 研究发现生成部署转录的模型身份解释了11.25%的测量方差并可重排模型排名；针对单一模型高性能优化的elicitation提示在其他模型上可能接近随机水平。EvalDetectBench通过校准方法修正了这些问题。

Conclusion: 评估意识是AI评估框架中不可忽视的因素，EvalDetectBench提供了可靠测量工具，同时为评估者提供检测特定基准可被识别程度的能力。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [120] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个条件性、方法论的框架，用以探讨未来若AI系统具备完整道德推理、道德意向性与道德反思能力时所引发的"AI自身伦理学"元伦理学问题，并区分了AI时代四个元伦理探究领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能的发展，传统以人类伦理为中心的元伦理学面临重构压力。现有研究多关注人类设计者对AI施加的伦理原则，但若未来AI系统展现出足够整合的道德能力，将产生区别于"外部施加伦理"的"AI自身伦理"问题，需要预先建立识别这些问题的框架。

Method: 论文采用条件性和方法论的分析路径：首先假设AI具备道德推理、道德意向性及道德反思等能力，在此基础上构建识别新兴元伦理问题的框架；随后将元伦理探究划分为四个领域（人类伦理与AI自身伦理各自从人类视角和AI视角进行的审视），并以现有主流元伦理理论（如认知主义/非认知主义、错误理论/成功理论、相对主义、客观实在论）加以检视。

Result: 论文辨识出AI时代元伦理学的四个探究领域，并论证了现有主流以人类为中心的元伦理理论在运用于AI自身伦理问题时难以直接迁移，需要实质性修订。

Conclusion: AI自身伦理学的出现将对当前元伦理框架施加显著压力，可能要求对这些框架进行实质性的精炼、重构或概念再造。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [121] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器解析成文法时不同解析器之间的分歧对形式逻辑推断的影响，提出了一个针对Duquene-Guines蕴含基的被动存活证书，通过Monte Carlo重放和Wilson置信下限来认证蕴含的稳健性，最终在密苏里州和印度中央法案数据上验证了其可用性但也揭示了其脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成文法越来越多地被机器解析，但不同解析器之间分歧严重（如密苏里州法规上数值阈值识别假阴性率达0.43），这威胁到基于解析结果的形式逻辑推断的可靠性。作者希望研究在存在此类噪声的情况下，哪些形式逻辑结论仍然能够存活。

Method: 构建一个被动的存活证书：（1）测量每个属性上解析器间的分歧；（2）在1,000次Monte Carlo试验中针对Duquene-Guines蕴含基进行分歧重放；（3）仅当单侧Wilson 95%置信下限达到0.95时才认证蕴含，每个认证蕴含附带前提跨度和最小反例。

Result: 在29,365个密苏里州条款和502个印度中央法案条款上，预先注册的留出测试通过（10个法规族精确匹配7个标题，16个在5%容差内匹配11个标题）；但在一种全局部署的错误模型下，93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限，2x2因子实验将该问题归因于校准率迁移而非选择偏差。

Conclusion: 该证书可用但脆弱：需要按章节校准或具备错误容忍度才能可靠部署。所有代码、数据产品和审计轨迹（包括一条已撤回的主张）均已公开。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [122] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过精确有限发现模型，将"信息共享改善汇总估计"与"消除独立救援行动"两种效应分离，并研究了注册式增量共享协议下的发现动力学、精确有界注册表的性质，以及在双智能体贝叶斯博弈中的共享均衡选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享对发现过程既可能通过改善汇总估计带来收益，又可能通过替代独立救援行动产生成本，以往研究未在有限模型中将这两种效应明确分离，本文旨在精确刻画两者的独立影响及相互条件。

Method: 采用精确有限发现模型，构建集中式行动-预算画像、注册式增量共享协议、精确有界注册表及双智能体贝叶斯博弈等理论框架，通过分析不同均衡结构来考察共享的利弊条件。

Result: 发现等精度个体可对应不同的组合价值；共享仅当汇总残差收缩快于独立救援尝试时才有益；注册表表现出压缩、聚合、中性曲线和有界零混合类等性质；在信号精度3/5下存在严格正共享区间，但结果具有均衡选择性而非普遍性。

Conclusion: 信息共享的价值并非天然存在，而是依赖于具体的协议结构和均衡选择；该结论为有限发现情境下的共享策略设计提供了理论依据，且模型为纯合成有限模型，不含真实数据。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [123] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算模型，将符号知识编码为结合自然语言与源代码的"心理程序"，并通过LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法顺序推断这些程序，以解释人类如何从稀疏、流式的噪声数据中增长抽象知识。该模型在多项行为研究中成功复现了人类归纳学习与主动探究的定量特征（如锚定效应、花园路径效应等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类如何从稀疏、流式且带噪声的经验数据中增长和维持抽象知识，是认知科学的长期挑战。任何计算模型需满足三个条件：数据与计算高效性、能表征不确定性以支持主动探究、足够灵活以表征人类可学习与思考的无限概念范围。已有模型（纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型）在这些方面各有不足。

Method: 该模型将符号知识编码为结合自然语言与源代码的"心理程序"，使用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法对这些心理程序进行顺序推断，从而融合语言式与程序式表征的优点，并借助LLM作为底层神经机制使推断既可行又可学习。

Result: 在多项行为研究中，该模型成功复现了人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量行为特征（如锚定效应、花园路径效应等）；相比之下，纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型要么无法完成底层任务，要么不能复现人类行为，要么需要过高的计算成本。

Conclusion: 结果表明，人类持续增长知识的一种可能方式是在心智中表征涵盖语言式和程序式表征的多种假说，并不断修订这些假说以近似贝叶斯更新；底层的神经机制（LLM）则使这种推断过程既可行又可学习。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [124] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"水合代理模式"（Hydration Proxy Pattern），解决企业平台转向对话式推理界面时，无状态LLM API所导致的会话状态与语义记忆管理负担问题，并通过"上下文稳定化指令"（Context Stabilization Mandate）来平衡主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业平台正在向对话式推理界面转型，但LLM API的无状态特性迫使客户端应用承担会话状态和语义记忆的全部管理负担。服务提供商的水平可扩展性与客户端会话管理需求之间存在架构上的鸿沟。

Method: 提出Hydration Proxy Pattern架构，将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦，确保平台对对话数据的主权，同时支持安全的多阶段语义接地。并进一步提出Context Stabilization Mandate来解决主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的矛盾。

Result: 论文阐述了Hydration Proxy Pattern如何实现平台对会话数据的主权控制与安全的多阶段语义接地，并提出了Context Stabilization Mandate作为解决状态管理与KV缓存权衡的机制。

Conclusion: 通过Hydration Proxy Pattern和Context Stabilization Mandate，弥合了无状态LLM API与企业对话平台之间的架构鸿沟，实现了会话数据主权保障与语义记忆管理的统一。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [125] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SSAKG 2.0，一个用于构建和操作结构顺序关联知识图谱（SSAKG）的开源软件包。该软件包采用Python与C混合实现，利用计算机内存的位级操作来高效搜索图连接，支持从部分无序上下文中重建完整序列。算法在随机数值序列、NLTK语料库句子派生的序列以及mRNA序列上进行了评估，并基于Apache 2.0许可证公开分发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要提供一个灵活高效的软件工具来处理大规模稀疏图，并支持从部分上下文重建完整序列的关联记忆功能，同时降低内存和计算开销。

Method: 采用Python实现高层编程环境，性能关键的图操作使用C语言实现并通过Python接口暴露。利用计算机内存的单个位来高效搜索图连接，SSAKG将对象表示为图顶点，有序序列表示为图的连接结构模式。

Result: 在随机生成的数值序列、NLTK语料库句子派生序列和mRNA序列上的实验表明，该软件包能够从部分上下文中存储和重建序列，同时为评估图密度、序列长度和记忆大小对检索性能的影响提供了基础。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0以Apache 2.0开源许可证分发，包含文档和可复现示例，并通过GitHub和Python包索引（PyPI）公开发布，为相关研究和应用提供了可访问的工具。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [126] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究持久记忆系统中过时存储事实覆盖当前权威证据的危害如何随模型能力变化而变化。使用冻结的闭集、动作评分基准（含Benefit和Safety两个套件），在Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B同族模型系列上评估。发现"记忆信任缺口"反映过度信任而非混淆：Benefit套件中所有规模模型均以0.92-1.00概率采用过时值；Safety套件中损害受能力门控，较大模型在过时笔记看似最新时崩溃更严重。2×2×2×2因子实验揭示触发过度信任的特征因特征和模型规模而异。缓解措施同样依赖能力：暴露元数据仅帮助大模型，预解决冲突才能恢复小模型精度。该模式在Llama-Instruct系列及外部数据集（RGB、MisBench）上重现。框架控制显示记忆标签无一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆支持个性化智能体，但过时的存储事实可能在无警告的情况下覆盖当前权威证据，造成有害后果。理解这种危害何时开始产生以及如何随模型能力变化至关重要，以便设计相应缓解措施。

Method: 采用3个部分：(1) 在冻结的闭集、动作评分基准上评估2个套件（Benefit套件无存储事实不可解，Safety套件权威工具始终持正确值），覆盖Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B同族模型系列；(2) 2×2×2×2因子实验分析触发过度信任的特征（标签移除、时效性、来源权威性、位置），并用直接跨规模对比检验克服区间重叠问题；(3) 验证缓解措施（暴露元数据vs预解决冲突）及在Llama-Instruct系列和RGB、MisBench外部数据集上的泛化，附加框架控制检验记忆标签效果。

Result: Benefit套件中所有规模模型均过度信任过时值（0.92-1.00概率）。Safety套件中Memory Trust Gap受能力门控，较大模型在陷阱条件下崩溃更多。因子实验发现：移除标签在各级放大过度信任；较新的过时日期欺骗大模型更严重；来源权威性弱且规模平坦；位置效应随规模由正转负。缓解方面，暴露元数据仅帮助大模型，预解决冲突可恢复所有模型精度，但两个较小检查点需要此项。模式在Llama-Instruct及RGB、MisBench数据集重现。框架控制显示无证据表明记忆标签被一致偏好——3个较小规模更信任过时文档，8B差异不显著。

Conclusion: 记忆带来的过度信任危害并非均匀分布，其主效应、触发特征和缓解策略均随模型能力规模而变化。这表明需要能力感知的持久记忆系统设计，而非一刀切的防护措施。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [127] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信念校准优化（BCO），将编码智能体优化器中隐式的环境响应信念显式写成持久化的上下文文档（世界模型），并在每轮评估后持续修订该文档。在五个基准上，BCO比缺少世界模型的对照方法取得更高的训练通过率，且在保留集上持续保持优势。换模型后BCO脚手架仍表现更优（除上下文窗口溢出场景），离线消融证明文档内容本身携带可复用的预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的性能依赖于围绕冻结模型搭建的脚手架。用编码智能体作为优化器时，每次编辑决策基于对环境的隐式信念（哪些出错了、哪种修改有帮助），但这种信念只存在于当前调用的推理中或潜伏在参数里，没有被写下来，后续调用无法利用这些信念，导致信息浪费与决策不一致。

Method: BCO将隐式信念显式化为一个持久化的上下文文档，作为环境如何响应编辑的世界模型，在每轮新候选被评估后持续修订该文档，将其加入标准优化循环中。

Result: 在五个基准（记忆问答、工具使用问答、代码即行动的应用智能体、终端智能体）上，BCO达到比匹配对照更高的训练通过率；差距在所有保留集上持续存在。换目标模型后BCO脚手架在测试任务上领先（除上下文窗口溢出导致未完成的情况）。离线消融显示，给定累积文档的新预测器比无文档或内容被伪造的同形式文档的预测器更准确地预测环境响应。

Conclusion: 将优化过程中的信念显式写下并持续修订作为世界模型，能有效提升脚手架的优化性能；世界模型的价值来自文档内容本身携带的可复用信息，而不仅仅是文档形式上的参与。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [128] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知Sybil问题"：多智能体AI系统中，看似独立的报告可能源于同一证据（共同祖先），而真正独立的证据也可能产生几乎相同的报告。作者证明仅基于报告文本的聚合器无法区分复制与独立佐证，并通过高斯共享根模型和超过2万次LLM智能体实验验证了推断应追踪证据祖先与依赖关系，而非智能体/报告的数量或相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统通过生成多个智能体报告来提升推断能力，但"多一个智能体并不等于多一个观测"——报告之间可能存在共同证据祖先导致的信息冗余，或由共享基础模型引发的相关提取误差，这使得系统的推断校准可能被严重高估。

Method: 作者形式化定义认知Sybil扩展（I(Theta; Z | R) = 0），证明报告生成的共同祖先与相似性会产生混淆。提出高斯共享根模型分析共同祖先对信息冗余的影响，建模相关提取误差，并进行超过20,000次受控LLM智能体报告/提取调用实验，包括多副本与多证据根的对照实验及可控的表示相似性操纵实验。

Result: 固定单一证据根时报告数从1增至32，朴素后验覆盖率从0.940骤降至0.263；报告数固定时证据根从1增至16可弥合差距且k=16时聚合器不可区分。智能体复制提取误差相关（gamma_cal=0.719，样本外估计）。表示相似性操纵使去重机制平均推断簇数变化1.425（95%CI [1.363,1.485]），而四倍真实祖先变化仅改变0.040（[-0.045,0.120]）。

Conclusion: 集体推断应追踪证据祖先与依赖关系，而非智能体/报告的数量或相似性。盲目增加报告数量会严重损害后验校准，报告空间的去重机制易被表示相似性而非真实祖先关系所混淆，需要开发追踪证据谱系的推断方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [129] [The Ceiling Is in the Channel: Auditing Learner Gaps and Measurement Frontiers in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01909)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Nusrat Jahan,Snehasis Mukhopadhyay,Shiaofen Fang,Vijay R. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套分离临床预测饱和两种来源的审计框架：通过"学习者差距"（learner gap）衡量模型未能提取已有信息的程度，通过"测量通道上限"（measurement-channel ceiling）衡量记录变量本身的信息边界。框架包含总变差分离下的最优平衡精度刻画、架构不变性、替换污染下的部分识别结果、交叉拟合上限估计器及多模态决策改进条件，并在三个真实队列及104个临床任务的综合综述中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测性能饱和可能源于两种截然不同的原因——机器学习模型未能榨干已有信息，或录入变量本身存在信息上限。现有研究无法区分这两者，导致"饱和"只是经验观察而缺乏可操作的决策依据：究竟是该换模型还是该加测量通道？

Method: 提出学习者差距与测量通道上限的分解框架；以最优平衡精度刻画总变差分离；推导替换污染下的部分识别结果、交叉拟合上限估计器及多模态改进的精确条件；添加标签置换乐观下限与欠拟合曲线两种有限样本诊断工具；在三个真实队列（UCI再入院、BRFSS糖尿病、NHANES HbA1c）上验证；并通过PRISMA引导的104个临床任务综述检验框架跨疾病类别的普适性。

Result: 在UCI和BRFSS中，调优的梯度提升几乎达到估计的信息前沿；在NHANES中，问卷与实测边缘前沿无差异但联合互补增益显著；所有队列中AUROC小幅提升的同时存在大幅贝叶斯决策翻转率差异；不同架构估计相似前沿但其平衡精度差异显著。104个任务的综述显示：存在广泛但非普适的结构化临床区域、同通道模型家族增益递减、跨测量通道变化性能更高的规律性。

Conclusion: 该框架将饱和从经验观察转化为可审计的决策：当学习者仍有提升空间（headroom）时改进模型；当达到测量通道上限时改进测量本身，而非盲目追求更复杂的模型。

Abstract: Clinical prediction can saturate for two different reasons: a fitted learner may fail to extract available information, or the recorded variables may impose a population frontier. We separate these quantities through the \emph{learner gap} and the \emph{measurement-channel ceiling}. Optimal balanced accuracy is characterized by total-variation separation, yielding architecture invariance, a sharp partial-identification result under replacement contamination, a cross-fitted ceiling estimator, and exact conditions for multimodal decision improvement. We add two finite-sample diagnostics, namely a label-permutation optimism floor and an underfit curve, and validate the audit on three real cohorts: UCI readmission ($n=99{,}343$), BRFSS diabetes ($n=253{,}680$), and NHANES HbA1c ($n=10{,}219$). Well-tuned gradient boosting nearly reaches the estimated frontier in UCI and BRFSS, whereas deliberately or practically deficient learners retain large gaps. NHANES yields a null difference between questionnaire and measured marginal frontiers but a significant joint complementarity gain, refining the simplistic claim that an objective modality must dominate. Across all cohorts, modest AUROC gains coexist with substantially larger Bayes decision-flip rates, and several architectures estimate similar frontiers while their achieved balanced accuracy differs sharply. A PRISMA-guided synthesis of 104 clinical tasks then shows that the same channel-level regularities recur across more than 18 disease categories: a broad but non-universal structured-clinical region, diminishing same-channel gains across model families, and higher performance when measurement channels change. The framework converts saturation from an empirical observation into an auditable decision: improve the learner when headroom remains; improve measurement when it does not.

</details>


### [130] [Looped Transformers under the Jacobian Lens: Does the Global Workspace Survive Recurrence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01924)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过Jacobian透镜和完整workspace测试套件，对比分析了循环深度变压器（Ouro-2.6B和Huginn-0125）与标准前馈变压器（Qwen3.6-27B）中的全局工作空间形成与访问方式，发现循环架构虽然能形成工作空间，但其内容访问模式与层叠架构存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现标准前馈变压器中存在一个中层带的、可言语化且具有因果效力的表示——即全局工作空间的功能类似物。但尚不清楚当深度通过循环而非堆叠不同层实现时，同样的工作空间功能是否会出现。循环和深度循环变压器通过跨深度复用相同权重，为这一问题提供了直接检验途径。

Method: 采用虚拟展开适配器将Jacobian透镜扩展至迭代架构；应用完整workspace测试套件（包括透镜拟合、读出和十一类因果实验家族）对三种模型进行测试：Ouro-2.6B（48层循环4次，深度监督）、Huginn-0125（4层核心循环16次，训练用于潜在推理），以及Qwen3.6-27B（64层未绑定层）作为标准基线。

Result: 发现每个架构的迭代部分均能形成工作空间，但循环改变了其访问方式：Ouro在每个循环中重构workspace内容，线性传输无法跨循环边界携带内容，写入和消融必须覆盖每个剩余循环；Huginn将内容向前传递全部16个循环，但读取、写入和消融仅在约两个循环的滑动窗口内起作用。新注入内容的可言语化程度与每次迭代的显式监督相关，而现有内容的可操控性则与之无关。

Conclusion: 循环架构确实能形成全局工作空间的functional类似物，但循环改变了工作空间的可访问性——不同架构类型（监督方式、循环次数）导致截然不同的访问模式，工作空间的形成与特定访问能力（可言语化vs可操控）之间存在可分离性。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [131] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将Qwen 4B指令微调模型进行端到端后训练量化（三元化）的过程，最终达到1.641有效比特/权重，但任务准确率从64.5%降至54.7%。打包可减少模型体积但作者不声称压缩能带来推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "1.58-bit"标签无法完整描述低比特语言模型的存储表示、能力保留和运行时行为，作者希望端到端研究实际转换效果，而不只是名义上的比特数。

Method: 对Qwen 4B指令微调模型采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ三元化和GPTQ风格误差补偿；权重仅量化，激活保持16位精度（省略ILA-AMP）。评估包括有效比特核算、能力保留、困惑度、校准敏感性、检查点组成和部署行为等。

Result: 最终具有1.641有效比特/权重量化，覆盖81.62%模型参数；十个能力比较中准确率从64.5%降至54.7%，不同任务衰减不均（BoolQ保留84.6%，ARC-Challenge仅保留43.8%）；困惑度在WikiText-2从13.639升至18.748。打包后模型从8.29 GiB降至3.96 GiB但困惑度基本不变；Triton微基准比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 超低比特量化会带来显著的且不均匀的能力损失，压缩不必然带来推理加速，有效比特核算和端到端评估比名义比特标签更重要。打包伪影未经端到端任务准确率和吞吐量基准验证。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [132] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Statistical Problem Formulation这一任务，将其分解为统计问题分类与变量识别两个子任务，并构建了包含1,013个样本、覆盖85个细粒度统计问题类别的StatFormBench基准。实验表明最优零样本模型在细粒度分类准确率仅达72.0%，变量集重叠度为63.2%，且无模型在两子任务上同时表现最佳，增强提示策略改进有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM统计评估大多假设分析目标已经明确指定，但实际用户常带着模糊目标和异构数据使用LLM，模型需要自行判断隐含的统计任务及相关数据。这一上游环节尚未被系统形式化和评估。

Method: 将Statistical Problem Formulation形式化为两个子任务：统计问题分类与变量识别及角色分配；基于五本跨领域统计教材和案例库构建StatFormBench，包含1,013个样本、20个粗粒度和85个细粒度问题类别；对14个开源与闭源LLM进行系统评测，并测试增强提示策略。

Result: 最佳零样本模型在细粒度分类上仅达72.0%准确率，变量集重叠度为63.2%；没有模型在两个子任务上同时表现最优；增强提示策略带来的提升有限或不一致。

Conclusion: 统计问题表述对现有LLM而言仍具挑战性，需要一个能够同时处理问题分类与变量识别的更强大模型或更有效的方法来应对这一上游统计任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap. No model performs consistently best across the two subtasks, while enhanced prompting strategies yield only limited or inconsistent gains. We release the benchmark data on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench and the evaluation code on GitHub at https://github.com/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench.

</details>


### [133] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM编码代理实现多组件数据系统进行了案例研究，记录了其5类缺陷，并在HotpotQA基准上验证了检索过滤策略的取舍：图约束实体集过滤在预算3即达到召回上限，而无过滤搜索在预算10时仅69%的时间能恢复全部所需证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理越来越多地执行端到端工程任务，但对其在系统级需求上的行为缺乏实证刻画，包括模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性以及检索过滤取舍等方面。

Method: 对一个LLM编码代理进行案例研究，令其根据预先制定的详细规范实现多组件数据系统（存储技术、模式、实体解析算法和检索过滤策略均预先固定）。在一次会话中编目5类缺陷，按违反的约束和检测方法分类。在公共HotpotQA基准上评估检索过滤策略（图识别实体集过滤vs无过滤搜索），用基准黄金证据标签替代实体识别，报告标准召回率，涵盖1至10的检索预算和100个问题、2994段文本池。

Result: 过滤式检索在预算3时达到召回上限（因为候选被限制在黄金段落本身），而无过滤搜索在预算10时仅69%的时间能恢复全部所需证据，该差距在所有预算水平均存在（符号检验p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: LLM编码代理的自主性在实现和交互设计中有成功之处也有需修正之处，包括一个声称的性能修复从未在触发它的回归问题上被重新测量的实例。该研究为系统级LLM编码代理行为提供了实证刻画。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [134] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClaimReceipt，一种面向智能体评估的声明相对收据规范与选择性验证器，通过绑定类型化交易证据到带签名的实验清单，对每条声明返回 PASS、INVALID 或 INCONCLUSIVE。在 1,392 条历史记录上复现全部人工审计结论，并在前瞻性 CR-3 实验中验证了覆盖率与账户核实功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估面临两个不同的证据问题：声明是否可从保留证据中重新计算（充分性），以及保留记录是否覆盖承诺的实验集（覆盖率）。通用日志和哈希链转录无法可靠回答这两个问题。

Method: 引入 ClaimReceipt 规范及选择性验证器，将类型化交易证据绑定到签名实验清单。在实现前冻结规范（SHA-256 18d109...b81），并设计 CR-2 历史验证与 CR-3 前瞻性实验两个验证阶段。CR-3 阶段在推理前提交30个任务，签署并链式连接终局收据，对审计方加密私有证据。

Result: CR-2 验证器复现全部5个人工标注审计结论，精确重放600条确定性与792条生成后记录，13个声明字段组均非冗余，11/11语义故障检测正确且0/8误报。CR-3 中完整证据获得覆盖率与账户核实通过，扣留终局收据返回 INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE。收据开销仅为推理时间的0.021%和每笔9.9KB。

Conclusion: 声明验证既需要声明充分的证据，也需要一个承诺的宇宙使遗漏可见。规范冻结并不能保证独立读者对其理解无歧义，仍需进一步可读性工作。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [135] [HeadWiseKV: Budgeted Per-Head Cache Residency for Hybrid Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02029)
*Renjie Xie,Juncheng Yang,Aoting Hu,Mingxi Zhang,Liyao Wu,Zheheng Hong,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeadWiseKV是一个无需训练的框架，通过为混合语言模型的残差全局KV缓存分配静态多级历史窗口来压缩缓存，在KV驻留预算约束下保持近Full-KV的下游质量，并显著减少峰值显存、扩展可服务上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中不断增长的KV缓存占用大量GPU显存并降低生成吞吐量，即使混合语言模型中残差全局注意力层仍主导上下文相关的缓存需求，现有方法缺乏对这一状态分配的系统研究。

Method: 提出HeadWiseKV框架，将压缩问题形式化为受限操作率-失真问题；核心策略生成算法SeqCalib按执行顺序逐层处理，并基于部署时的低层策略条件化决策以建模跨层交互；通过分组缓存运行时将所选策略实现为实际按头KV驻留，而非覆盖完整缓存的掩码。

Result: 在四个混合长上下文模型上，HeadWiseKV保持近Full-KV的RULER和LoCoMo质量；在固定模型系统研究中，112K上下文长度下采样峰值设备显存降低8.59%，最大验证成功的上下文从114K扩展到161K。

Conclusion: HeadWiseKV通过训练前可预测的静态每头缓存驻留策略，在不牺牲本地、循环和线性路径的情况下有效压缩混合语言模型的KV缓存，同时兼顾质量、显存效率与服务能力扩展，为混合架构模型的长期上下文部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference retains a growing key--value (KV) cache during decoding, which consumes substantial GPU memory and can reduce generation throughput. This bottleneck remains in hybrid language models because their residual global-attention layers can dominate context-dependent cache demand. We study how to allocate this state under an aggregate KV-residency budget. We introduce HeadWiseKV, a training-free framework that compresses the residual global KV caches of hybrid language models while preserving their native local, recurrent, and linear paths. It assigns each physical KV head a static, multilevel history window, making cache demand predictable before serving. We formulate this allocation as a restricted operational rate--distortion problem and propose SeqCalib as the core policy-generation algorithm in HeadWiseKV. SeqCalib processes layers in execution order and conditions each decision on the lower-layer policy used at deployment, thereby accounting for interactions across depth. A grouped-cache runtime materializes the selected policy as actual per-head KV residency rather than a mask over a full cache. We evaluate downstream quality across four hybrid long-context models and study physical residency and serving behavior on Qwen3.6-27B. HeadWiseKV retains near-Full-KV RULER and LoCoMo quality across the evaluated models. In the fixed-model systems study, it reduces sampled peak device memory by 8.59\% at a 112K context length and extends the largest verified successful context from 114K to 161K.

</details>


### [136] [Monitoring Web Agents Without Internal Signals: Observable Trajectories and Key-Step Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02057)
*Sitong Pan,Yipeng Shen,Yilin Lu,Caiwen Ding,Lu Cheng,Qianwen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在无法获取模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）的情况下，如何利用可观测的轨迹信号对Web智能体进行前缀级别的风险预测，判断当前执行是否偏离轨道或趋于失败。作者设计了宏观特征（跨步智能体-环境行为与反馈）和微观特征（意图、动作与预期状态变化的一致性度量），并将首个未被纠正且导致最终失败的关键错误作为标签边界。在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web基准上的实验表明，可观测信号与内部信号基线表现相当，且支持早期干预和跨网站类别的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型内部不确定性信号（如token logits）不可用时，Web智能体的可靠性监控面临困难。现有方法依赖内部信号，但实际部署中许多黑盒模型不公开此类信息，因此需要探索仅基于可观测轨迹信号的风险预测方法。

Method: 提出两种可观测轨迹表示：宏观特征总结跨步骤的智能体-环境行为与反馈；微观特征通过重复黑盒查询度量意图、动作与预期状态变化之间的一致性。标签策略不直接继承最终结果标签，而是将观测延续中第一个未被纠正且与最终失败相关的关键错误标记为关键步骤边界，从而保留失败轨迹中有效的前缀为"在轨"。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web两个基准上，使用五种开源和闭源主干模型，可观测轨迹信号的表现与使用内部信号的基线方法相当。预测器还支持在固定误切预算下的早期干预，并能迁移到未见过的网站类别。

Conclusion: 可观测的轨迹信号能够提供有价值的Web智能体风险预测能力，在缺乏模型内部信号的实际场景中具有实用意义，并支持早期干预和跨领域泛化。

Abstract: Reliable web-agent monitoring is difficult when model-internal uncertainty signals such as token logits are unavailable. In this work, we study prefix-level risk prediction for web agents using observable trajectory signals: given an evolving prefix, estimate whether the current execution remains on track or is tending toward failure. We derive two observable trajectory representations: Macro features summarize cross-step agent--environment behavior and feedback, while Micro features measure the consistency of intention, action, and anticipated state change through repeated black-box queries. Instead of inheriting the final result label, we label the first critical error that remains uncorrected in the observed continuation and is associated with final failure as a key-step boundary, preserving valid early prefixes of failed trajectories as on track. Across WebArena-Lite and Online Mind2Web web agent benchmarks with five open- and closed-source backbones, observable trajectory signals are competitive with internal-signal baselines. The resulting predictors also support early intervention under fixed false-cut budgets and transfer across held-out website categories. These findings show that observable trajectory signals support valuable risk prediction abilities.

</details>


### [137] [DocHop: Benchmarking Out-of-domain Multi-hop Reasoning in Information-Dense Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02059)
*Zhuoran Yu,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Jaden Park,Xinyi Gu,Zexue He,Soochahn Lee,Rogerio Feris,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DocHop基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在文档风格图像中进行图表-上下文集成推理的能力。实验显示最佳模型准确率仅62.83%，与人类90%以上的准确率存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常将图表理解和文档问答分开评估，忽略了模型能否利用文本上下文来决定如何选择、解释和聚合图表证据这一关键能力。

Method: DocHop通过随机逻辑优先的生成流程构建，具有可控制的推理深度和视觉密度，包含2,074个样本，覆盖六个任务类别。文档叙述指定多步组合约束，图表提供对应数据值，问题基于叙述中的语义参考标签，要求模型先解析目标实体再跨多图表聚合证据。

Result: 在多种专有和开源MLLM上的实验显示：人类标注者准确率超过90%，而最佳模型仅达62.83%；推理增强模型表现一致提升，但性能随推理复杂度增加而下降。

Conclusion: DocHop为多跳文档推理提供了一个受控测试平台，揭示了现有MLLM在集成图表-上下文推理方面与人类表现之间的显著差距，表明这一方向仍具较大挑战。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on structured visual understanding tasks such as chart and document question answering. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these domains in isolation, leaving underexplored a key capability: whether models can use textual context to determine how chart evidence should be selected, interpreted, and aggregated. We introduce DocHop, a benchmark for integrated chart--context reasoning in document-style images.
